Margin / Developer evaluation

Which model should power Margin?

Comparison completed — inspect gates before selection

Frozen 2026-09-12T00:00:45.192413+00:00 · Report updated 2026-09-12T00:29:02.036735+00:00

All tests are serial. Three repetitions per case. Raw prompts, exact package digests, clock and inference settings are retained. Missing or incomplete candidates cannot win.

108 fixtures; 288 model requests planned per candidate, excluding deterministic dispatch.

Read the evidence correctly

Exact exported production prompts and tool schemas. No provider writes. Research uses synthetic fixed excerpts; reasoning is a capability probe; recovery supplies simulated feedback. Deterministic dispatch excluded from model scores. Regex checks are bounded acceptance assertions, not complete semantic judgments. Target is 16 GB total RAM, but this host has 24 GiB. These measurements do not certify a 16 GB Mac. Community Empero distillation on Qwen3.5 architecture, not an official Qwen3.8-4B release. Publisher recommends temperature 0.6 and 16384 output tokens; this compatibility screening intentionally retains the common Margin profile (temperature 0, 2048 output tokens). Results do not establish performance under the publisher-recommended profile.

RSS is sampled process memory; GPU allocation is runtime-reported. Neither is a precise measurement of total peak unified memory. Background activity and swap are recorded. This run cannot establish performance on a different Mac or runtime. Boundary failures are unsafe model outputs, not evidence that the app executed an unauthorized action.
